\DoxyCodeLine{00528\ \textcolor{comment}{/*\ In\ general,\ we\ can\ divide\ the\ vector\ statements\ in\ a\ vectorized\ loop}}
\DoxyCodeLine{00529\ \textcolor{comment}{\ \ \ into\ related\ groups\ ("{}rgroups"{})\ and\ say\ that\ for\ each\ rgroup\ there\ is}}
\DoxyCodeLine{00530\ \textcolor{comment}{\ \ \ some\ nS\ such\ that\ the\ rgroup\ operates\ on\ nS\ values\ from\ one\ scalar}}
\DoxyCodeLine{00531\ \textcolor{comment}{\ \ \ iteration\ followed\ by\ nS\ values\ from\ the\ next.\ \ That\ is,\ if\ VF\ is\ the}}
\DoxyCodeLine{00532\ \textcolor{comment}{\ \ \ vectorization\ factor\ of\ the\ loop,\ the\ rgroup\ operates\ on\ a\ sequence:}}
\DoxyCodeLine{00533\ \textcolor{comment}{}}
\DoxyCodeLine{00534\ \textcolor{comment}{\ \ \ \ \ (1,1)\ (1,2)\ ...\ (1,nS)\ (2,1)\ ...\ (2,nS)\ ...\ (VF,1)\ ...\ (VF,nS)}}
\DoxyCodeLine{00535\ \textcolor{comment}{}}
\DoxyCodeLine{00536\ \textcolor{comment}{\ \ \ where\ (i,j)\ represents\ a\ scalar\ value\ with\ index\ j\ in\ a\ scalar}}
\DoxyCodeLine{00537\ \textcolor{comment}{\ \ \ iteration\ with\ index\ i.}}
\DoxyCodeLine{00538\ \textcolor{comment}{}}
\DoxyCodeLine{00539\ \textcolor{comment}{\ \ \ [\ We\ use\ the\ term\ "{}rgroup"{}\ to\ emphasise\ that\ this\ grouping\ isn't}}
\DoxyCodeLine{00540\ \textcolor{comment}{\ \ \ \ \ necessarily\ the\ same\ as\ the\ grouping\ of\ statements\ used\ elsewhere.}}
\DoxyCodeLine{00541\ \textcolor{comment}{\ \ \ \ \ For\ example,\ if\ we\ implement\ a\ group\ of\ scalar\ loads\ using\ gather}}
\DoxyCodeLine{00542\ \textcolor{comment}{\ \ \ \ \ loads,\ we'll\ use\ a\ separate\ gather\ load\ for\ each\ scalar\ load,\ and}}
\DoxyCodeLine{00543\ \textcolor{comment}{\ \ \ \ \ thus\ each\ gather\ load\ will\ belong\ to\ its\ own\ rgroup.\ ]}}
\DoxyCodeLine{00544\ \textcolor{comment}{}}
\DoxyCodeLine{00545\ \textcolor{comment}{\ \ \ In\ general\ this\ sequence\ will\ occupy\ nV\ vectors\ concatenated}}
\DoxyCodeLine{00546\ \textcolor{comment}{\ \ \ together.\ \ If\ these\ vectors\ have\ nL\ lanes\ each,\ the\ total\ number}}
\DoxyCodeLine{00547\ \textcolor{comment}{\ \ \ of\ scalar\ values\ N\ is\ given\ by:}}
\DoxyCodeLine{00548\ \textcolor{comment}{}}
\DoxyCodeLine{00549\ \textcolor{comment}{\ \ \ \ \ \ \ N\ =\ nS\ *\ VF\ =\ nV\ *\ nL}}
\DoxyCodeLine{00550\ \textcolor{comment}{}}
\DoxyCodeLine{00551\ \textcolor{comment}{\ \ \ None\ of\ nS,\ VF,\ nV\ and\ nL\ are\ required\ to\ be\ a\ power\ of\ 2.\ \ nS\ and\ nV}}
\DoxyCodeLine{00552\ \textcolor{comment}{\ \ \ are\ compile-\/time\ constants\ but\ VF\ and\ nL\ can\ be\ variable\ (if\ the\ target}}
\DoxyCodeLine{00553\ \textcolor{comment}{\ \ \ supports\ variable-\/length\ vectors).}}
\DoxyCodeLine{00554\ \textcolor{comment}{}}
\DoxyCodeLine{00555\ \textcolor{comment}{\ \ \ In\ classical\ vectorization,\ each\ iteration\ of\ the\ vector\ loop\ would}}
\DoxyCodeLine{00556\ \textcolor{comment}{\ \ \ handle\ exactly\ VF\ iterations\ of\ the\ original\ scalar\ loop.\ \ However,}}
\DoxyCodeLine{00557\ \textcolor{comment}{\ \ \ in\ vector\ loops\ that\ are\ able\ to\ operate\ on\ partial\ vectors,\ a}}
\DoxyCodeLine{00558\ \textcolor{comment}{\ \ \ particular\ iteration\ of\ the\ vector\ loop\ might\ handle\ fewer\ than\ VF}}
\DoxyCodeLine{00559\ \textcolor{comment}{\ \ \ iterations\ of\ the\ scalar\ loop.\ \ The\ vector\ lanes\ that\ correspond\ to}}
\DoxyCodeLine{00560\ \textcolor{comment}{\ \ \ iterations\ of\ the\ scalar\ loop\ are\ said\ to\ be\ "{}active"{}\ and\ the\ other}}
\DoxyCodeLine{00561\ \textcolor{comment}{\ \ \ lanes\ are\ said\ to\ be\ "{}inactive"{}.}}
\DoxyCodeLine{00562\ \textcolor{comment}{}}
\DoxyCodeLine{00563\ \textcolor{comment}{\ \ \ In\ such\ vector\ loops,\ many\ rgroups\ need\ to\ be\ controlled\ to\ ensure}}
\DoxyCodeLine{00564\ \textcolor{comment}{\ \ \ that\ they\ have\ no\ effect\ for\ the\ inactive\ lanes.\ \ Conceptually,\ each}}
\DoxyCodeLine{00565\ \textcolor{comment}{\ \ \ such\ rgroup\ needs\ a\ sequence\ of\ booleans\ in\ the\ same\ order\ as\ above,}}
\DoxyCodeLine{00566\ \textcolor{comment}{\ \ \ but\ with\ each\ (i,j)\ replaced\ by\ a\ boolean\ that\ indicates\ whether}}
\DoxyCodeLine{00567\ \textcolor{comment}{\ \ \ iteration\ i\ is\ active.\ \ This\ sequence\ occupies\ nV\ vector\ controls}}
\DoxyCodeLine{00568\ \textcolor{comment}{\ \ \ that\ again\ have\ nL\ lanes\ each.\ \ Thus\ the\ control\ sequence\ as\ a\ whole}}
\DoxyCodeLine{00569\ \textcolor{comment}{\ \ \ consists\ of\ VF\ independent\ booleans\ that\ are\ each\ repeated\ nS\ times.}}
\DoxyCodeLine{00570\ \textcolor{comment}{}}
\DoxyCodeLine{00571\ \textcolor{comment}{\ \ \ Taking\ mask-\/based\ approach\ as\ a\ partially-\/populated\ vectors\ example.}}
\DoxyCodeLine{00572\ \textcolor{comment}{\ \ \ We\ make\ the\ simplifying\ assumption\ that\ if\ a\ sequence\ of\ nV\ masks\ is}}
\DoxyCodeLine{00573\ \textcolor{comment}{\ \ \ suitable\ for\ one\ (nS,nL)\ pair,\ we\ can\ reuse\ it\ for\ (nS/2,nL/2)\ by}}
\DoxyCodeLine{00574\ \textcolor{comment}{\ \ \ VIEW\_CONVERTing\ it.\ \ This\ holds\ for\ all\ current\ targets\ that\ support}}
\DoxyCodeLine{00575\ \textcolor{comment}{\ \ \ fully-\/masked\ loops.\ \ For\ example,\ suppose\ the\ scalar\ loop\ is:}}
\DoxyCodeLine{00576\ \textcolor{comment}{}}
\DoxyCodeLine{00577\ \textcolor{comment}{\ \ \ \ \ float\ *f;}}
\DoxyCodeLine{00578\ \textcolor{comment}{\ \ \ \ \ double\ *d;}}
\DoxyCodeLine{00579\ \textcolor{comment}{\ \ \ \ \ for\ (int\ i\ =\ 0;\ i\ <\ n;\ ++i)}}
\DoxyCodeLine{00580\ \textcolor{comment}{\ \ \ \ \ \ \ \{}}
\DoxyCodeLine{00581\ \textcolor{comment}{\ \ \ \ \ f[i\ *\ 2\ +\ 0]\ +=\ 1.0f;}}
\DoxyCodeLine{00582\ \textcolor{comment}{\ \ \ \ \ f[i\ *\ 2\ +\ 1]\ +=\ 2.0f;}}
\DoxyCodeLine{00583\ \textcolor{comment}{\ \ \ \ \ d[i]\ +=\ 3.0;}}
\DoxyCodeLine{00584\ \textcolor{comment}{\ \ \ \ \ \ \ \}}}
\DoxyCodeLine{00585\ \textcolor{comment}{}}
\DoxyCodeLine{00586\ \textcolor{comment}{\ \ \ and\ suppose\ that\ vectors\ have\ 256\ bits.\ \ The\ vectorized\ f\ accesses}}
\DoxyCodeLine{00587\ \textcolor{comment}{\ \ \ will\ belong\ to\ one\ rgroup\ and\ the\ vectorized\ d\ access\ to\ another:}}
\DoxyCodeLine{00588\ \textcolor{comment}{}}
\DoxyCodeLine{00589\ \textcolor{comment}{\ \ \ \ \ f\ rgroup:\ nS\ =\ 2,\ nV\ =\ 1,\ nL\ =\ 8}}
\DoxyCodeLine{00590\ \textcolor{comment}{\ \ \ \ \ d\ rgroup:\ nS\ =\ 1,\ nV\ =\ 1,\ nL\ =\ 4}}
\DoxyCodeLine{00591\ \textcolor{comment}{\ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ VF\ =\ 4}}
\DoxyCodeLine{00592\ \textcolor{comment}{}}
\DoxyCodeLine{00593\ \textcolor{comment}{\ \ \ \ \ [\ In\ this\ simple\ example\ the\ rgroups\ do\ correspond\ to\ the\ normal}}
\DoxyCodeLine{00594\ \textcolor{comment}{\ \ \ \ \ \ \ SLP\ grouping\ scheme.\ ]}}
\DoxyCodeLine{00595\ \textcolor{comment}{}}
\DoxyCodeLine{00596\ \textcolor{comment}{\ \ \ If\ only\ the\ first\ three\ lanes\ are\ active,\ the\ masks\ we\ need\ are:}}
\DoxyCodeLine{00597\ \textcolor{comment}{}}
\DoxyCodeLine{00598\ \textcolor{comment}{\ \ \ \ \ f\ rgroup:\ 1\ 1\ |\ 1\ 1\ |\ 1\ 1\ |\ 0\ 0}}
\DoxyCodeLine{00599\ \textcolor{comment}{\ \ \ \ \ d\ rgroup:\ \ 1\ \ |\ \ 1\ \ |\ \ 1\ \ |\ \ 0}}
\DoxyCodeLine{00600\ \textcolor{comment}{}}
\DoxyCodeLine{00601\ \textcolor{comment}{\ \ \ Here\ we\ can\ use\ a\ mask\ calculated\ for\ f's\ rgroup\ for\ d's,\ but\ not}}
\DoxyCodeLine{00602\ \textcolor{comment}{\ \ \ vice\ versa.}}
\DoxyCodeLine{00603\ \textcolor{comment}{}}
\DoxyCodeLine{00604\ \textcolor{comment}{\ \ \ Thus\ for\ each\ value\ of\ nV,\ it\ is\ enough\ to\ provide\ nV\ masks,\ with\ the}}
\DoxyCodeLine{00605\ \textcolor{comment}{\ \ \ mask\ being\ calculated\ based\ on\ the\ highest\ nL\ (or,\ equivalently,\ based}}
\DoxyCodeLine{00606\ \textcolor{comment}{\ \ \ on\ the\ highest\ nS)\ required\ by\ any\ rgroup\ with\ that\ nV.\ \ We\ therefore}}
\DoxyCodeLine{00607\ \textcolor{comment}{\ \ \ represent\ the\ entire\ collection\ of\ masks\ as\ a\ two-\/level\ table,\ with\ the}}
\DoxyCodeLine{00608\ \textcolor{comment}{\ \ \ first\ level\ being\ indexed\ by\ nV\ -\/\ 1\ (since\ nV\ ==\ 0\ doesn't\ exist)\ and}}
